# UI slice S2 record: B's stock screen

Branch `claude/ui-abc-s2`, from main `a4b201fa` (the merge of slice S1, PR #148; its tree equals the S1 head `9402b05d`). One
section per package; it grows with each package. Every number is tagged **実測** (measured here, with its command) or
**未検証** (not measured). "The harness" is the round-3 measurement harness and "the yardstick" the S1-P0 tools as S2-P0
re-based them (state matrix, values, screenshots, ledger, phrases, surfaces, timing); both are scratch tools outside the
repository, never committed. bd is not installed on this machine, so each package is tracked here instead.

**The base of every S2 comparison is the S1 head `9402b05d`** (S2-P0's baselines below), not main `01066ebe`: a values,
ledger or surfaces difference against those baselines that is not in the allow-list below is a failure.

Open owner questions this slice does not decide (each keeps the shipped behaviour; S1's record has their numbers): the market
band (S1 question 1); the breakout row's name (the plan's 上抜けの維持 or the shipped 上抜け維持（確定分）, printed through the one
constant `BREAKOUT_HOLD_NAME`); O2-b on Today; the waiting headline's tag; 鮮度未確認 with an open guide; plan §9's O3, O3-a,
O3-b, O4, O9, O10, O11 and BEH-2; and S1's two §5.3 departures (S1 owner questions 13 and 14).

   added in S2-P2's record after the S2-P1 round 3 verdict counted three) and `:18` cites `brief-decision.spec.js:23`
   (`EARNINGS_SOON` is at `:26` on the S2-P1 head; all three read again on `4920e3af`). Correct the three in that package's own
   commit, citing the mutator names (`below50Rows`, `pressure`, `EARNINGS_SOON`) rather than line numbers, since
   `brief-decision.spec.js` has already moved in S2; no separate commit for this alone. The package that does it marks this item
   done here. Still carried after S2-P2 (stopped; no commit that stays on the branch edits that file).
2. **From S2-P2's round 1 verification (minor), for any later attempt at the strip** (after the owner's O10 ruling): the title's
   qualifier loses 試算のみ. The level tiles print 試算のみ when `plan.trialOnly` is set (`StockVerdict.jsx`, `sizing`, on the head);
   the strip of `a2593468` offers only 参考・地合い 0% / 参考・地合い未確認 / 参考 / 既定条件, so a trial-only plan would lose its
   qualifier, while plan S2-P2 lists 試算のみ among the qualifiers the title carries on a 50% / 25% day. Found by reading the
   diff (not run in a render: 未検証).
3. **From the same verification (minor), for any later attempt at the strip**: the plan asks for a one-line title row every
   day, but S2-P2 measured it two lines at 320 with either 参考・地合い qualifier (296.7 / 312.0px against 262px of line; the
   verification did not re-measure), and the second line starts with 「・」.

edited, and no file under `frontend/src` changed except one comment. Verified (pass) on `045d715a`; its two round 1 orders
(record and scratch only) are done at the end of this section.

### The matrix extension (plan §5.2's S2 stock states that exist without new code)

786 view-modes (375: 275, 390x844 fold: 275, 1440: 236), from 660. Every new view has a reach check (the tree's own
modelMarket, the conclusion's `data-gate` as the S1 head gives it, a text the page must show, the page clock, the late age in
the bar, the pressed period), so a recipe that silently does not take fails its view. The fixture stays frozen: every recipe
is a mutator in the scratch state table.

| State | Stock | How it is opened (scratch mutator) | `data-gate` on the S1 head (published / 0% / unknown / late at 50%) | Reach text |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| a stale pivot (below it) | ZZAR | the pivot 40% over the price (canonicalPivot drops a level more than 25% away) | stalePivot / stalePivot / stalePivot / stalePivot | 「旧ピボットから −29%」 |
| no pivot | ZZAR | no `se_pivot_price` and no `vcp_pivot` | unjudged in every market | 「ピボットを確認できないため位置は未確認です」 |
| a `vcp_pivot` fallback price | ZZNV | the pivot moved to `vcp_pivot` (the row and its SEC detail) | touch / marketHold / marketUnknown / touch | 「VCP（日次）」 |
| a four-digit price with a live pivot | ZZNV | the whole stock ×11 (the row's and the detail's price, pivot and audit levels, the chart's bars and its row copy; the last close equals the price, $1,218.14) | touch / marketHold / marketUnknown / touch | 「$1,218.14」 |
| the late publication at 50% | every earlier stock state but the 0% marketHold one (its late form is the matrix's late-below50 stock view) | the late clock (10/5 and 10/6 closed, nothing published) | each state's published gate (D18 keeps every gate) | the bar's 「2営業日前」 |
| the periods 3か月, 1年, 週足 | the governing states of S2-P2: the buyable-day zone stock (ZZNV), the 0% marketHold stock (ZZNV), the earnings-soon stock (ZZNV) | the period as the tab's session state | touch / marketHold / earnings, as at 6か月 | the pressed period |

Already in the matrix since S1 and kept: the earnings-soon gate, a weekly-pattern pivot (ZZHB), a four-digit price with a stale
pivot (ZZNV at $1,234.56), a sell chip the call does not state (ZZNR), `stopPercent` 5 and an applied structural stop (ZZSD),
each under the 0% and the unknown market; they gain the late publication at 50%. Still a gap: stop reached and an
analysis-day breakout (S2-P6 adds them). Screenshots of the new views were not taken (not ordered in S2-P0; the shots tool
can take them from the same state table).

### The live-gate proof (実測)

With the S2 allow-list empty:

- **The S2 head equals the base in every yardstick section.** The S2 head `b8d95b05` (this package's code commits and its first
  record commit), served on port 4461: the full values run, **786 passed** (1.1 h, no re-run), 0 unreached, 0 page errors, 0
  notes; its `values.json` and `matrix.json` are byte-identical to the S1 baselines (the same sha256 as above), so the values
  diff reads **0 differences, 0 failures** (matrix 511 compared, 0 differences). The cross-surface oracle: 19 passed (5.8
  min), `surfaces.json` byte-identical, **0 differences**. The ledger: 1845 lines, **0 differences**. The phrase scan:
  byte-identical, 0 new hits. (The first 13 minutes of this run shared the machine with the S1 head's harness runs on port
  4462; the byte identity shows they changed nothing.)
- **A broken copy fails.** A throwaway worktree of the same commit (removed afterwards), with two plants left uncommitted
  and run with the tool's dirty-tree flag: (1) the no-pivot sentence gains 「（植）」 (`StockVerdict.jsx`), which only the new
  no-pivot views show; (2) the verdict sentence's exposure is the cap × 80 (`marketTabModel.js`: 「最大 40%」 where every other
  surface says 50%).
  - Values, the four no-pivot views, ZZNV's zone view and Today (18 view-modes, 18 passed), against the S1 base with the
    empty list: **exit 1, 80 failures**. Every no-pivot view-mode (four markets, 375 / 390x844 / 1440) loses the old
    sentence and gains the planted one in its closed and opened lines and its first fold; every 1440 view gains
    「…最大 40%…」 and the number 40% in place of 50%. The control, ZZNV's zone view at 375 and 390x844: 0 rows.
  - The cross-surface oracle on the m50 and below50 states: **exit 1**. m50's gate figure line and verdict block at 1440
    read 最大40% (`open/最大50% -> open/最大40%`), and the agreement flags figureLoose and figureStrict turn false. below50, the
    control: no row (the 17 states not run are listed as missing).
  - The ledger reads a commit, not the working tree, and this package makes no commit outside the S2 branch, so its plant
    is offline: in a copy of the S1 ledger SWKS's plan stop × 0.999 (forced 50% market). The ledger diff: **exit 1**, 1
    failure (`plan.stop`). The ledger run's own sensitivity was shown in S1-P0 with this tool's same bytes.

### Deviations from the order

- **The late publication at 50% is added to every earlier stock state, not only the S2 states the order lists** (all but the
  0% marketHold state, whose late form the matrix already has): S2-P1's market line is on every stock screen, and a waiting day
  (D18) is where it differs most. +51 view-modes, about 5 minutes per run.
- **A second four-digit state.** The four-digit price the matrix had (ZZNV at $1,234.56) has a stale pivot, so no pivot,
  zone, stop or plan number in it is four-digit; S2-P2's fit ladder and D8's stop comparison need one that is: the whole of
  ZZNV ×11 ($1,218.14 with its live pivot) is added beside it.
- **No 25% market for the stock states** (not in the order; plan §8 names it for the critics): the strip's 25% qualifier is
  for S2-P2's own spec to pin.
- **Both known dead entries of S1's list removed too** (the order allowed keeping them): they match nothing for the same reason
  as the 22, a superseded form.
- **The ledger's negative check is offline** (above): its run reads a commit, and the order allows commits only on the S2
  branch.
- **The S1 head's cross-surface run was repeated once** (a Playwright timeout in `pend-m50`, re-run from the start: 19
  passed).
- **The S1 head's harness runs and the S1 rebuild shared the machine with the S2 head's values run** (ports 4462 and 4461):
  every output equals its known value or its S1 twin byte for byte.
- **The comment-only code commit is typed `docs(frontend)`**: it changes a comment and nothing that is built.
- The harness's rebuild of the S1 head (298 MB) is kept for later packages' render-probe and sell-evaluation bases.

commits, `66d2b543` and `9c466b34`, and this record (the last subsection of this section).

### What shipped (plan S2-P1, D2, D5, D17, D18, D19)

- **Line 1, the market line** (`StockVerdict.jsx`, `marketCallLine`): read from `marketBand()` (the band Today's gate reads) with
  the analysis date, 14px, in the text ink on every day (the subject 地合い and each kind label in the secondary text ink; no
  tone): 「地合い 10/2：検討可（独自判定）・目安 最大50%（独自設定）」 / 「…最大25%…」 / 「地合い 10/2：買いは控える（独自判定）・目安
  0%（独自設定）」. With an open guide once a newer session has closed (pending or late), the 更新待ち form of D18, from
  `verdictWaiting` with the same `marketUpdateState().target` as Today's gate and the 市場 tab: 「地合い 10/2時点の判定・更新待ち
  （独自判定）・目安 最大50%（10/2時点・独自設定）」 (the waiting answer carries the date, so the subject takes no second one; the
  figure stays, labelled with the date as Today's figure is). A 0% day keeps its words on a waiting day. An unknown market:
  「地合い 10/2：未確認・買いは見送り」 (no figure, no %, no kind). The line is made of units (the subject with the date, each word of
  the answer with its kind, the figure with its kind) that a line breaks only between, after a 「：」 or a 「・」 (S1-C1's whole-unit
  approach: inline blocks), so a kind label or a figure never splits.
- **Line 2, labelled 銘柄**: the label 「銘柄」 (14px) on its own line over buyGate's own title (20px, as on main) and its line
  (13px), all unchanged, the dot and the card's tone as before. The label takes no width from the title's line (the title pins
  of brief-decision.spec.js hold unchanged).
- **Short forms on the 銘柄 line** (D5): a sell chip the call does not state (every gate but `broken` and `failedBreakout`) and a
  near earnings date (`earningsCall.soon`) under any gate but `earnings` join the label in their chip's own words: 「銘柄・保有なら
  売りのサイン」 (ZZNR), 「銘柄・決算まで2日」 (an earnings-soon stock on a 0% day), 「銘柄・50日線の下」.
- **The tiles, the meter and the chips leave the card** (D5): the three level tiles (unchanged in content and qualifiers) are a
  block of their own (`brief-levels`) right after the chart card, where S2-P2's strip goes; the chips (same chips, order and
  tones) are a block under it (`brief-call-chips`); the pivot meter is not drawn on the stock screen any more, and its one
  sentence for an unknown pivot, 「ピボットを確認できないため位置は未確認です。」, is a full-width line under the tiles, word for word.
- **Plumbing**: `StockView` builds the band (`marketBand(market, { date, target })`); `StockScreen` and `BriefApp` pass BriefApp's
  own `target` (both call sites: the phone's stock screen and the 1024px+ pane). The prop defaults to null (the open form), and the
  browser spec pins the waiting form at 390 and in the 1440 pane.

### Readings of the plan taken here (stated, not decided for the owner)

- **「both lines 14 px, the 銘柄 title as on main」**: read as the market line and the 銘柄 label at 14px, the gate's own title kept
  at main's 20px and its line at main's 13px. The other reading (the title at 14px) would change every title pin of
  brief-decision.spec.js and the call's own words' weight; nothing in the plan asks for that.
- **The 銘柄 label on its own line, over the title.** Inline before the title it takes width from the title's line (the label
  and a gap are about 34px), and the title pins (one line with 6px of slack at 375 on ZZHW / ZZZF and on the dated breakout,
  one line at 320 and 375 on the touch, two lines at 320 on the RS-only miss) would move. On its own line it costs one 14px
  line (21px) and no title moves. The short forms (D5) join that line, so they cost no further line.
- **The waiting form's date.** The plan writes 「地合い M/D：…」 for the open and closed forms; the 更新待ち answer already carries the
  date (「10/2時点の判定・更新待ち」), so the subject takes no second one there. The figure keeps its date in its label
  (「目安 最大50%（10/2時点・独自設定）」), as Today's gate labels its figure 「新規買いの目安（10/2時点・独自設定）」 (D18: 「the figure
  stays, labelled 「M/D時点」」).
- **The kind labels' ink.** The whole line is in the text ink (D17); the subject 地合い and the two kind labels are in the secondary
  text ink at the same 14px, as row heads and kinds are elsewhere. No tone, on any day.
- **The unknown form.** marketBand's answer 「未確認・買いは見送り」 with the analysis date, no figure, no 「%」 and no kind (the band
  names no kind when it states no verdict; Today's gate prints no kind beside its unknown headline either).
- **The short forms' words.** A sell chip's own short text (`sellChipView(...).text`: 保有なら売りのサイン, 50日線の下, 崩れ（独自判定）
  on a template miss, 売りのサイン 未確認 …) and the earnings chip's own words (決算まで3日, 決算当日, 決算日経過), never a new phrase.
  「売りのサイン n件」 is the chip's own text only when the chip itself says it.

### What the first fold reads on a late 50% day with a zone stock (S1 owner question Q11; 実測, the values run's fold of `stock-touch@late`)

```
375x812 (the bar, then the first fold top to bottom)      390x844
ZZNV $110.74 ↑+2.0%                                        ZZNV $110.74 ↑+2.0%
2営業日前・Novalux Semiconductor (synthetic)・Technology    2営業日前・Novalux Semiconductor (synthetic)・Technology
地合い 10/2時点の判定・                                      地合い 10/2時点の判定・更新待ち（独自判定）・
更新待ち（独自判定）・                                       目安 最大50%（10/2時点・独自設定）
目安 最大50%（10/2時点・独自設定）                           銘柄
銘柄                                                       10/2にピボット到達
10/2にピボット到達                                          ピボット比 ±0.0%・50日線から +11.1%。ピボット
ピボット比 ±0.0%・50日線から +11.1%。ピボッ                 から＋5%までが買いの範囲です。
トから＋5%までが買いの範囲です。                             3か月 6か月 1年 週足（58週）
3か月 6か月 1年 週足（58週）                                 10/02 始110.85 … (the readout, the chart and its tags)
10/02 始110.85 … (the readout, the chart and its tags)
```

(`stock-touch@late` and `late-m50/stock-ZZNV` read the same.) So on such a day the first line under the bar says, before
anything about the stock, that the verdict is 10/2's and a newer session has closed, with the figure dated 10/2; the
call under it is the buy-zone call (data-gate `touch`, the green edge and dot) with its buy-range sentence.

The call's title, its tone (the green edge and dot of a buy-zone call), its line and every buyGate sentence are as on a fresh
day: only the market line says the verdict is the 10/2 one and a newer session has closed. The bar above it carries the late
age 「2営業日前」 in the warning tone, as since S1. Whether the call itself should also hold its green on such a day is not
decided here (S1 Q11 stays open for the owner with this reading; plan O9 covers the call card's own tone).

### The proof behind the allow-list (実測; a scratch generator, offline, over the full values run)

Every difference of every view-mode is one of the package's stated changes, or the view fails:

- **lines**: the only lines added are the six market-line forms (open 50% / 25%, 0%, the 更新待ち form at 50% / 25%, the unknown
  form) and the 銘柄 line in three forms (「銘柄」, 「銘柄・保有なら売りのサイン」 on ZZNR, 「銘柄・決算まで2日」 on the earnings-soon stock on a
  0% day), one of each per view-mode that shows a stock; the only lines removed are the meter's nine (its four names and its five
  ticks), once each and only all together (291 view-modes). The stale-pivot and no-pivot views lose nothing.
- **numbers**: exactly the number tokens of the added lines in and of the meter's ticks out, per view-mode.
- **names**: only the meter's accessible name 「ピボットからの位置…」 leaves; nothing is added. **svg, pairs and the dock**: unchanged in
  every view-mode (the tiles' label/value pairs bind as before: their markup is still `.brief-kgrid`).
- **order**: in every view-mode the page's lines, less the meter's run and the two new lines, are the S1 head's with one block (the
  tiles and the chips, which ended right before the chart card's 「3か月」) moved to after the chart card (the unknown-pivot sentence
  set aside: it moved from the meter's place to under the tiles).
- **first fold** (375 / 390f): every entered visual line's words are the S2 view's own content and every left line's the S1 view's:
  the fold changed place, never content.
- Only stock screens and the 1440 views with a stock pane change; the matrix's checked fields are equal everywhere.

### Plan §5.3 departures (for the conductor to accept or refuse)

§5.3's expected S2 list names, for removal, the meter's accessible name and its tick labels. This package's list also holds,
each as exact strings with anchored view lists:

1. **The market line** (six forms) and its number tokens (10, 2, 50%, 25%, 0%): §5.3's S2 list does not name it, though plan
   S2-P1 and D2 ship it and §5.3's oracle names it as a surface 「from S2」.
2. **The 銘柄 line** (「銘柄」 and its two short forms, and 「2日」): plan S2-P1 / D5 ship it; §5.3 does not list it.
3. **The meter's four segment names** (土台形成中, ピボット接近, 買いゾーン, 買いゾーン超過): scale names, not values, retired with the
   meter (D5); §5.3 names only its ticks and its accessible name.
4. **The order and first-fold moves** (lineOrder, leftFirstFold, enteredFirstFold, foldOrder) of the tiles, the chips and the
   chart card (D5, D20).

### Deviations from the order

- **Two entry files changed** (`BriefApp.jsx`, `StockScreen.jsx`; the plan lists only stock-chunk files): the stock screen needs
  the same `target` Today's gate reads, and only BriefApp holds it. One prop through both call sites; entry +0.01 kB gzip.
- **The order lives in `StockVerdict`** (a `chart` slot: card, chart, tiles, chips), not in `StockView`'s own JSX: the call is
  computed once and its three blocks are rendered around the chart card. The model (`stockCall`, `marketCallLine`) is not
  exported (a component file exporting functions would add lint warnings); the vitest reads it through the rendered blocks.
- **The 銘柄 label is its own line** and **the waiting form takes no 「M/D：」 lead** (both above, with their reasons).
- **The tiles' block is a plain block, not a card**, and the chips' block too: S2-P2 replaces the tiles with the strip attached to
  the chart card; the tiles keep their own backgrounds and qualifiers.
- **Tests outside the plan's list changed**: `brief-invariants.spec.js:379`, `brief-sell.spec.js:162`, `brief-plan.spec.js:266,
  346` (readers of the moved blocks; table above). `brief-invariants.spec.js:125-238` needed no change.
- **The cross-surface oracle's per-state timeout went from 240 s to 480 s** (a tool change, re-pinned): with eight surfaces per
  state (two of them stock screens that wait for the chart), two runs on the S1 tree each timed out once on a late clock while
  the S2 values run shared the machine (`late-below50`, then `late-m25`: the same timeout twice, not a flake). The third run, with
  480 s, passed 19 of 19.
- **The values run needed `--resume` twice** (the session's background limit stopped the first run; one Playwright timeout on a
  Today view was re-run once), so `values.json` is built from three runs of the same commit; `meta.json` records the last.
- **The surfaces oracle's first S2 run used the tool before its timeout change**; the run with the final tool gave the same bytes.
- **The unknown-pivot sentence keeps its words but not its look** (実測, a scratch probe of `stock-nopivot@pub` on both trees,
  light and dark, 320 / 375 / 390 / 1440). Inside the card it took the card's paragraph rule: 13px in `--text-2`, two lines at
  320 (263 px of line). Under the tiles it is a plain note: 12px in `--text-3`, one line at every width (288 px at 320). 12px is
  the size plan S2-P2 gives the strip's full-width lines, where this sentence goes next; the sentence has no digit. Its contrast
  on the page ground is 4.82:1 light and 8.27:1 dark (from the theme tokens; 7.69 / 10.46 before, on the card).
- **A resume.** A container restart cut the first full static suite at 702 of 886 tests (only the host baseline
  `brief-invariants.spec.js:243` had failed by then); the record was committed first, and the suite, its race-free copies,
  lint, vitest and the probe were then run again on the committed head (the table above).

### Not measured here (未検証)

- Timing (stock open, period switch) against the S1 head: not run. The package removes the meter's DOM and moves blocks; no chart
  or data path changed (the probe is identical relative to the card). S2-P8 owns it: it must run stock-open and period-switch
  timing interleaved against the S1 tree in both orders (S1 first and S2 first), on an idle machine, after a base-against-base
  noise run.
- `counts.json`: not re-run (the checks table says why: no module of the snapshot's entry and no backend file changed).
- Screenshots of the matrix views (the probe's few PNGs were looked at; the shots tool was not run).
- CI and Design Acceptance: nothing is pushed by this package. Of the moved or retired blocks, Design Acceptance reads only
  `[data-testid="brief-conclusion"]` (kept; `design-review.mjs:339, 545, 565`); neither it nor `brief-design-contract.mjs` names
  `.brief-meter`, `.brief-kgrid`, `.brief-tags` or `.brief-tilequal` (a grep, not a run).
- A real phone's fonts: every fit is measured with the CI fonts. The tightest fits are the waiting line at 390 with 12/28 (6.1 px
  of slack on its first line) and the 0% line at 375 with 12/28 (33.1 px).
- The market line on a calendar-less bar (鮮度未確認, target null): the open form, by the shipped rule (S1 Q9 stays open).

- **R6**: S2-P1 (plan D5): the pivot meter is retired from the stock screen (its segment names, its tick labels −10% … +10% and its accessible name ピボットからの位置; ピボット比 stays in the readout and the call's line)

## S2-P2 The 4-number strip

Status: **stopped at its owner line (O10).** Started from `437d73c1` (tree clean). The strip was built and committed
(`a2593468`), measured on that commit, then reverted (`4920e3af`): `git diff 437d73c1 HEAD -- frontend` is empty, so the branch
ships exactly what S2-P1 left. The plan's ledger, applied in its order, is not enough: in two of the strip's own states the stalls
screen is over its 3124px line at 375x812 (below), so plan S2-P2's rule ("still over: stop and report per state") applies. No
cap, budget, token, waiver, build target, Design Acceptance tool, frozen fixture or frozen spec was edited; `positionSizing.js`
was not edited in either commit. The revert's commit message was amended once, right after `git revert --no-edit`, to add the
reason and the two trailer lines; no other commit was touched.

### What `a2593468` built (reverted; kept in the history for the owner and a later attempt)

- `StockStrip.jsx` (new, stock chunk) in the tiles' place, right after the chart card: the title 「試算（買いの推奨ではありません）」 and
  its qualifier (「参考・地合い 0%」 on a 0% day, 「参考・地合い未確認」 with an unknown guide, else 参考 for a plan the gate leaves a reference
  only, 既定条件 at default settings, or none; 「試算」 only in the title); four `dt`/`dd` cells (so the yardstick binds them without a
  `PAIR_CELLS` row): ゾーン上限 (the value, 「+5%」) | ピボット (the value and its day; 未確認 with the unknown-pivot sentence) | 損切り
  (「損切り −7.0%」, the plan's stop, 「買値 X」; 収縮の中 as the stop cell's own fourth line, in text ink) | 株数 (n株 / 1株未満 / `—`
  未計算, and リスク $625, 上限N% or the 1株未満 reasons); the value fit ladder (16px, then 14px, then 2 x 2, measured per width with
  `scrollWidth <= clientWidth`, a value never clipped); the full-width lines, each only in its state: 前提 (the full form), 「52週高 X
  （+n%）」 (the published audit's high of the analysis date on certified bars, the chart's own reading, when it lies in the zone; n is
  the high over the close, which gives minor B9's +2.9% on its example), the cap form 「上限25%・実リスク $590」, the unknown-pivot
  sentence word for word.
- The pivot's day reads `pivotDay` (new, exported from `StockVcpCard.jsx`), the one helper `pivotDayNote` now builds the VCP card's
  sentence from (the card's text byte for byte the same), so the strip's date and the card's are one string; 日付未確認 otherwise.
  Measured on the working tree before the commit (the same model and markup; the CSS then had the cell gap of 8px and the wider
  padding), not in the head run: ZZHB (the weekly-pattern oracle) 「9/22（週足の型）」, ZZNR 「10/1」, the no-pivot state 未確認; in the head
  run ZZNV reads 日付未確認 in every view (its Setup Engine pivot is not the current one).
- `stopLevel.js` (new): `provisionalStop(pivot)` (pivot × (1 − 7/100)), `stopPrinted`, `stopLevels(plan, pivot)` (the two stops as
  printed strings and whether they are the same), used by nothing on screen yet: the stop row is S2-P6's. The blocked-plan line
  「計画なし（買値を置けない状態）・仮の損切り X…は売りのカード」 needs that row and was not rendered.
- `brief-strip.css` (new, loaded with the stock chunk; never `.brief-kgrid`): labels, notes, title and lines 12px, values 16px mono,
  text inks only (D17); attached to the chart card (the stack's gap taken back, the card's bottom corners square, one shared
  outline). The ledger's steps (below).
- `StockVerdict.jsx`: the tiles' block and its model left; the strip takes the call's `referenced`; `PIVOT_UNKNOWN_NOTE` moved to
  the strip and is re-exported from its old path. `StockView.jsx` passes the plan's `settings`.
- Build of `a2593468` (the harness's `run-build.sh`, archive of HEAD; 実測): `npm run build` entry **113.02 kB gzip** (line 134.00;
  S2-P1 113.00); Design Acceptance entry `index-Dz1jHT9x.js` 112.80; `StockView` 24.81 → 25.72 kB; `priceChart` 18.58; the strip's
  CSS is its own asset (`StockView-*.css`), listed in `precache-manifest.json`.

### Why it stops (実測: a scratch geometry probe on `a2593468`, tree clean, CI-faithful: Chrome for Testing 147 headless shell, the CI fonts, Node 22, port 4461; the S1 tree on 4462; `content` = the bottom of `#brief-main .brief-stack`, the specs' measure)

The ledger, in the plan's order (each column cumulative; L0 is the strip as designed, with the cell gap of 6px that keeps
「損切り −7.0%」 (73.7px, measured on the head) on one line in a 375px cell: with an 8px gap the cell is 73.0px and the label wraps,
+14px (measured on the working tree before the commit). L0 and L1 are the committed build with CSS injected by the probe to undo
the later ledger steps, not builds of their own; L3 is the commit as it is):

1. **The strip's own padding** (L1): padding 4/6 → 2/2 and the two inner gaps 4 → 2 (a 2px floor: no text touches a border).
2. **The 前提 line's short form**: buys **0px** at every phone width, so it is not taken. Its text is 368.4px against 262 / 317 / 332px
   of line at 320 / 375 / 390 (the full form is 422.3px); both forms are two lines on every phone. Measured with and without
   `font-feature-settings: "palt"`: the same widths (the CI fonts have no proportional forms).
3. **The call card's row gap** (L3): 10px → 0 below 521px (between the market line and the 銘柄 line).

## S2-P3 One name per level; chart gutter; placer checks

Status: **done.** Started from `a5c07ac2` (tree clean; its frontend tree `f0364941…` is `437d73c1`'s, the S2-P1 ship). Three
code commits, in this order, each measured on its own: (A) `006efa46` the gutter's style (CSS only), (B) `ca74ec67` the zone
label's rename, (C) `a5928646` the placer fix for B7, which reproduced in the app's own placer (its vitests in `chartLabels.test.js`
and `priceChart.test.js`); A7 did not reproduce. Record commits: `b451a9f5` and `dcc8d32f` (each written before the run it states),
`4956f484` (CONTEXT.md) and this section's results. The values, ledger and surfaces yardstick holds against the S1 baselines with 149
new allow-list entries (0 failures), the static suite fails only its two host baselines, and no owner line moves. No cap, budget, token, waiver, build target, Design Acceptance tool, frozen fixture or frozen spec is
edited; `positionSizing.js` is not touched.

### Readings of the plan taken here (stated before the work; the conductor accepts or refuses each)

- **The renamed label is the priced one only.** 「買いゾーン 〜X」 becomes 「ゾーン上限 X」 (plan S2-P3). The placer's fallback when
  no spot is free for the priced label, the bare 「買いゾーン」, stays: it names the green band, not a level, and its rows include
  mid-band and above-band spots, where a bare "upper limit" would point at the wrong height. Counts on the S2 head's probes (実測,
  `probe-dc92755`, the S2-P1 ship's chart): priced 337 / unpriced 4 / none 151 of the 492 label views; priced 90 / unpriced 3 /
  none 39 of the 132 oracle label views; the bd20584 render of the 11-12 rebuild has 34 unpriced zone labels in 476 renders
  (the S1 head's own render is the base below).
- **Names that already hold:** the legend says ピボット and 損切り; the chart's stop name is 「損切り −n.n%」; the readout's 「対ピボット」
  (「ピボット」 below 1024px) is ピボット比, a distance, not a level; its 「終」 is the close in the 始・高・安・終 row, the short form
  minor B1 itself allows (「終 443.68」 when space is short), and lengthening it would move the readout (the chart card is pinned at
  508 exactly). The sell card names no level but ピボット. The plan card's stop is 「−1R X」 with 損切り for screen readers (the R
  bar's own scale); its heading names the entry as ピボット or 終値. None of these is changed.
- **Left open, outside this package's files:** 「買いの範囲」 on S2-P1's interim level tiles (`StockVerdict.jsx`, owned by the
  stopped S2-P2); the chart's accessible name says 「損切りの目安 X」 (a rename would add accessible-name entries on every stock view
  with a stop; not done here, listed for S2-P8); the four-digit gutter tag prints the price without cents (`tagText`, a box rule).
- **C5, the gutter (style only):** the level tags (ピボット, 損切り, 安値, a level named at the axis) become plain text in their
  level's ink with no plate; the 終値 tag (`bc-lasttag`) is the one filled plate. The tag markup, `data-tag`, every `<rect>` and
  every class stay; only fills and strokes of the plates and the inks of the tag texts change (`brief.css`, `brief-chart.css`).

### Expected diffs, stated before any probe or render run of this package's commits

Bases: the harness probe and oracle probe of the S2 head's chart, `probe-dc92755.json` (sha256 `fdc9cec1…`, = `probe-9bc1419`;
since `dc927555` only `StockVerdict.test.jsx` changed under `frontend/src`) and its oracle file; the render probe of the 11-12
rebuild on the S1 tree `9402b05d` (its own build, port 4462: no chart file changed in S1 or S2 before this package); the
yardstick's `s1/` baselines with the S2 allow-list.

- **Commit A (gutter).** Harness probe and oracle probe: **`cmp`-identical** to `probe-dc92755(-oracles).json` (they record texts,
  classes, boxes, the texts' halo (paint-order, stroke, stroke width), opacity, font, the solids' boxes, masks, the aria label and
  the card's HTML texts; no fill). Render probe: in every render, **only the tag texts' `fill`, `contrast`, `tagContrast` and
  `plate`** (level tags: `plate` and `tagContrast` null, `fill` the level's ink; the 終値 tag: `plate` the filled ink, its text
  the ground ink). Values yardstick: no difference.
- **Commit B (rename).** Harness probe against commit A's (= `probe-dc92755`): in each label view whose zone label is priced
  (337 of 492; 90 of 132 oracle views), that one text's `name` and its box: `x1`, `bb` and `reserved` (`data-box`) exactly 12.0px
  narrower by `width()` (「〜」 counts 12px), its `x0` kept where its spot is kept, and its entry in `halos`; in each values view
  that draws it (328 of 480; 90 of 132), the `svg` entry's text. **Nothing else.** The placer can still move other things, so
  these classes are named now and every view in one is explained one by one or fixed: (i) the zone label takes another spot (its
  candidate grid depends on its width); (ii) a label placed after it (T numbers, depths, the averages' names and leaders,
  上抜け, the notices), or one moved by `repair()`, takes another spot; (iii) a view whose zone label is the unpriced 「買いゾーン」
  today turns priced, the narrower priced label now finding a spot. Render probe against commit A's: the same text and box change
  for the zone text, under the same three classes. Values yardstick against `s1/`: in every stock view-mode that draws the priced
  label, the old text leaves and 「ゾーン上限 X」 enters in `closed` / `opened` `lines`, `svg` and `seq` and in the first fold where
  it is above the dock (a fold line holds every text on its visual row, so such a fold line changes as a whole); the number tokens
  do not change (X is the same string). Each such difference becomes an exact allow-list entry (no regex text), with its views.
- **Commit C (placer fixes)**, if any: stated in its own subsection before its run.

### The placer checks (stated before the run)

- **A7, the stop's name by its line.** In every render (the 476 targets and the extra targets below) that draws a stop line: the
  stop's name is drawn (not hidden; a name carried to the legend is counted apart), it lies in the plot (its right edge at or left
  of the plot's, never over a gutter tag), and the nearer edge of its glyph box is at most `LEADER_OFF_LEVEL` (4.5px) from its
  line (the app's first rows put it 3px over or 5px under by the estimate, 1-2px in the tight rows, on the line when cut). It
  reproduces A7 if a view has that edge more than 4.5px off (the app's second-row fallback at −21 / +23 would be 15 / 17px, A7's
  shape) or a stop name over a tag.
- **B7, a T number by its contraction.** B's remedy (start each T label at its bracket's left edge) has no object in the app: the
  app draws no bracket lane and centres each T number on its contraction's box; left-aligning would move every T label, which the
  stated probe diff rules out. The app's form of the check: in every render, each T number (alone or in 「Tn −d%」) overlaps its own
  box horizontally, lies within the 30px band its search uses above or below that box, and is no nearer (centre to centre,
  across) to another contraction's box than to its own. It reproduces B7 (INGM's T5 under the pivot's flag) if a T number fails
  one of these. 「T1・T2 は省略」 has no counterpart (the app draws every contraction), and the readout already prints the 50/150/200
  values (the dropped-tag half of B7).
- **Where they run:** a scratch copy of the harness's render probe that also records the contraction boxes, the T numbers and
  depths with their contraction, the stop names and the tag boxes (the harness's own script is not edited), on the 476 targets and
  on extra targets the 476 lack: INGM, LIND and OGN (B7's stocks) at 320 / 390 / 1440, and SWKS and HUM at 390 again. The
  harness's own `run-render.sh` runs too, for the base-to-head comparison.

### Commit C, stated before its run: a T number never centred over another contraction's box (B7 reproduced)

**What reproduced (実測, before any code of C).** The placer checks on the S1 tree's render of the 11-12 rebuild (`9402b05d`,
port 4462; 476 renders and 44 extra renders of INGM, LIND and OGN at 320 / 390 / 1440 and SWKS and HUM at 390; a scratch copy of
the harness's render probe that also records the contraction boxes, the T numbers and depths, the stop names and the tag boxes):

- **A7 does not reproduce.** 416 renders draw a stop line (104 draw none); in all 416 the stop's name is drawn (none hidden, none
  carried to the legend), lies in the plot and over no tag, and the nearer edge of its glyph box is 0.0-4.0px from its line
  (0.0: 26, 0.1: 1, 1.0: 18, 2.0: 158, 2.1: 7, 3.9: 1, 4.0: 205); 105 of them are over candles with their halo. Nothing to fix.
- **B7 reproduces.** Of 439 T numbers, 166 fail the check stated above. Two different things are in that count: a T number
  beside its own box over no other box is the placer's design (its search is "inside it or beside it"), but **70 T numbers are
  centred over another contraction's box and not over their own** (39 at 320, 27 at 390, 4 at 1440), where they read as that
  contraction's number. INGM 3か月 at 390 is one of them: 「T5 −7%」 is centred 26px left of its 8px box, over another box, as in
  B7. That class is the one C fixes; the other failures of the stated check (a T number beside its box over no box) are left as
  designed and are counted in the record, not fixed.

**The rule.** The placer gains one option, `allow` (`chartLabels.js`): a spot it rejects is never taken, candle-free or over
candles, in `place()`, in `repair()` and so in `settle()`. `priceChart.js` passes it to the T number's searches (alone above its
box, or the combined 「Tn −d%」 around its box): a spot is allowed when the label's centre is over its own box or over no box. The
depth under a box and the combined label's last fallback (the whole plot on a coarse grid, which keeps a depth from being left
out) are not held to it; labels placed by that fallback are counted apart. Vitests: the option in `chartLabels.test.js`, and in
`priceChart.test.js` a chart whose middle contraction's column is a wall of candles (it fails without the rule: T2 is centred over
a neighbour's box at 262px wide).

**Expected diff of C, against commit B's runs (stated before C's run):** in the harness probe and oracle probe, only label views
in which a T number (or 「Tn −d%」) takes another spot, and in them the labels placed after it (the averages' names and their
leaders, 上抜け, the notices) or moved by `repair()` (a level's name, the zone's label), and the cuts and masks that follow those
labels; the fixed geometry (candles, lines, averages, tags, solids, contraction boxes, the card, its HTML texts, the aria label)
identical in every view; the values views identical but for the order of the averages' names where their retry order changes.
The render probe against commit B's: the same classes.

**Cut rule (C ships only if all hold; else it is reverted and B7 is reported as reproduced and unfixed, with its numbers):**
1. On the rebuild render (476 + the extra renders), the T numbers centred over another contraction's box and not their own fall
   to **0**, outside labels placed by the whole-plot fallback (counted apart).
2. No contraction loses its T number or its depth (missing labels): no increase against commit B on the rebuild render, the 492
   label views and the 132 oracle views.
3. Forced T numbers and depths (over candles with their halo) rise by at most the number of T numbers the rule moved; the rise
   is reported.
4. `labelAudit` (collision-free) stays green at 320 / 375 / 390 / 1440 (`brief-chart.spec.js`); `priceChart.identity.test.js`
   and every chart vitest stay green.
5. No owner line moves: the chart card's height is identical in every probe view (the card is fixed-size; checked, not assumed).

### Commit C's cut rule, measured (実測, renders of the 11-12 rebuild on `ca74ec67` and `a5928646`, the renderer's own geometry: a T number's reserved box against the contraction boxes' x and width)

| Check | B (`ca74ec67`) | C (`a5928646`) | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|
| T numbers centred over another contraction's box, not their own, placed around their box | 69 (25 of them over candles) | **0** | 0 — holds |
| the same, placed by the whole-plot fallback (counted apart) | 2 | 8 | reported |
| labels placed by the whole-plot fallback in all | 3 | 12 | reported |
| contractions without their T number / without their depth | 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 | no increase — holds |
| forced T numbers (over candles, with their halo) | 72 | 76 | at most +69 — holds (+4) |
| forced depths | 0 | 0 | holds |
| `brief-chart.spec.js` (labelAudit at 320 / 375 / 390 / 1440, every chart spec) | — | **53 passed** | green — holds |
| chart vitests (`priceChart`, `priceChart.identity`, `chartLabels`, `StockChartCard`, `PriceChart`) | 143 passed | **145 passed** | green — holds |
| chart card box in the 972 probe views | identical | identical | holds |

C ships. **What stays for the conductor:** the whole-plot fallback (which keeps a depth from being left out) is not held to the rule,
and C sends 9 more combined labels there (3 → 12), 8 of them centred over another contraction's box and away from their own (HAFC
週足 320 T6, RMBI 1年 / 週足 320 T6, DRI 1年 / 週足 320 T2, INGM 週足 320 T6, RDNT 1年 390 T2, TRST 週足 390 T2): 71 misattributed T numbers
become 8, all in that fallback. Holding the fallback to the rule too (with the unrestricted grid as a last resort) is the obvious next
step; it was not stated before the run, so it is not in C. The other kind of failure of the B7 check (a T number beside its own box,
over no box: 142 in B, 183 in C) is the placer's design and is not changed. A7 does not reproduce on any of S1, B or C (416 of 416).

### Deviations from the order (for the conductor to accept or refuse)

- **The expected probe diff did not hold literally for commit B.** Stated: in each priced zone-label view, that one label's text and
  box width, "nothing else". Measured: in 129 of the 337 priced label views the narrower label takes another spot (most often the band's
  top row, its first choice) and labels placed after it move with their cuts and masks; 2 views turn the unpriced 「買いゾーン」 into the
  priced form. All are in the three classes named in the record before the run; none is unexplained; none changes a fixed element
  (candles, lines, tags, boxes, the card).
- **A third code commit (C)** beyond the plan's rename and gutter: the plan orders "fix only what reproduces"; B7 reproduced (70
  T numbers centred over another contraction's box), so C is that fix, stated with its cut rule before its run. Its residual (8 T
  numbers placed by the whole-plot fallback over another box) is reported above.
- **The plan's B7 wording ("a T label starts at its bracket's left edge") is checked in the app's own form** (a T number over its own
  box, or over no box), stated before the run: the app centres T numbers on their boxes and draws no bracket lane.
- **The render probe ran on the S1 rebuild's data for every head** (the S1 head's rebuild output, linked, not copied): no input of the rebuild
  (backend, `frontend/tools`, the model modules) changed in S2 (`git diff --name-only 9402b05d HEAD`), so the rebuild is the same;
  the rebuild's own `counts.json` is re-run below.
- **The plan's test lines for this package** (`brief-chart.spec.js:856,874,974,1019`, `brief-plan.spec.js` stop words) are other
  tests on this head; no static spec asserts a changed chart word, so none was edited (the four new gutter tests are additions).
- **`brief-call.spec.js` is not edited**, so the carried order on its three stale citations stays carried.
